Entry 1
Category: Atomic Structure
Concept: Chemistry
Definition: The branch of science that studies matter, its properties, composition and reactions.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in chemistry.
- Widely used in science, medicine and engineering.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Chemistry is an important topic in chemistry.
Related Concepts:
Atomic Structure, Periodic Table, Chemical Bonding, Organic Chemistry, Physical Chemistry

Entry 2
Category: Atomic Structure
Concept: Atom
Definition: The smallest unit of an element that retains its properties.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in chemistry.
- Widely used in science, medicine and engineering.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Hydrogen is the simplest atom with one proton and one electron.
Related Concepts:
Atomic Structure, Periodic Table, Chemical Bonding, Organic Chemistry, Physical Chemistry

Category: Reactions & Applications

- Fundamental concept in chemistry.
- Widely used in science, medicine and engineering.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Combustion of methane.

Atomic Structure, Periodic Table, Chemical Bonding, Organic Chemistry, Physical Chemistry

Category: Reactions & Applications
Concept: Endothermic Reaction

- Fundamental concept in chemistry.
- Widely used in science, medicine and engineering.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Photosynthesis.

Category: Physical & Organic Chemistry
Concept: Oxidation
Definition: Loss of electrons or increase in oxidation state.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in chemistry.
- Widely used in science, medicine and engineering.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:

Concept: Ion
Definition: An atom or molecule with a net electric charge.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in chemistry.
- Widely used in science, medicine and engineering.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Na⁺ and Cl⁻ form sodium chloride.
Related Concepts:
Atomic Structure, Periodic Table, Chemical Bonding, Organic Chemistry, Physical Chemistry
